                                      11 *01*  DESCRIPTIVE-NAME = SCHEDULE SERVICE ROUTINE                       00119802

                                      13 *01*  COPYRIGHT = NONE                                                  00139902
                                      14 *01*  STATUS = VS2, RELEASE 2.0                                         00146402
                                      15 *                                                                       00146602
                                      16 *01*  FUNCTION = THE FUNCTION OF THIS MODULE IS TO COMPLETE THE         00159902
                                      17 *     SCHEDULING OPERATION INITIATED BY THE SCHEDULE MACRO. IT MOVES    00169902
                                      18 *     SRBS TO THE APPROPRIATE SERVICE PRIORITY LIST (SPL) AT THE        00170302
                                      19 *     SPECIFIED PRIORITY LEVEL. IT ALSO HANDLES OTHER FUNCTIONS         00170702
                                      20 *     INCLUDED IN INTRODUCING DISPATCHABLE WORK INTO THE SYSTEM,WHICH   00171102
                                      21 *     INCLUDES:                                                         00171502
                                      22 *        1. ALERTING A CPU DISPATCHED TO THE WAIT ADDRESS SPACE THAT    00171602
                                      23 *        THERE IS WORK TO BE DISPATCHED.                                00171702
                                      24 *        2. CALLING THE MEMORY SWITCH ROUTINE WHEN MAKING A SWAPPED-IN  00171802
                                      25 *        ADDRESS SPACE READY.                                           00173302
                                      26 *        3. CALLING THE SRM WHEN A SWAPPED-OUT ADDRESS SPACE IS         00175302
                                      27 *        READY.                                                         00177302
                                      28 *                                                                       00177502
                                      29 *02*     OPERATION =                                                    00179002
                                      30 *        GLOBAL SCHEDULE-                                               00181002
                                      31 *          1. INPUT SRBS ARE QUEUED TO THE GLOBAL SPL.                  00183002
                                      32 *          2. IF THE WAIT ADDRESS SPACE IS DISPATCHED,THEN EVERY CPU    00183402
                                      33 *          DISPATCHED TO THE WAIT ADDRESS SPACE WILL BE SIGNALLED VIA   00183802
                                      34 *          THE RPSGNL FUNCTION.                                         00184202
                                      35 *                                                                       00184602
                                      36 *        LOCAL SCHEDULE -                                               00184702
                                      37 *          1. INPUT SRBS WILL BE QUEUED TO THE CORRECT LOCAL SPL.       00184802
                                      38 *          2.  IF AN SRB IS BEING SCHEDLED TO AN ADDRESS SPACE THAT IS  00184902
                                      39 *          ON THE IN-CORE QUEUE, THEN MEMORY SWITCH WILL BE INVOKED.    00185302
                                      40 *          3. IF AN SRB IS BEING SCHEDULED TO A SWAPPED-OUT ADDRESS     00185702
                                      41 *          SPACE,  THEN A SYSEVENT WILL BE ISSUED TO NOTIFY THE         00185802
                                      42 *          SRM THAT THE ADDRESS SPACE CAN BE SWAPPED IN.                00185902
                                      43 *                                                                       00186102
                                      44 *                                                                       00186502
                                      45 *01*   NOTES =                                                          00186602
                                      46 *                                                                       00188002
                                      47 *02*     DEPENDENCIES =                                                 00189502
                                      48 *        1. THE DISPATCHER LOCK MUST BE HELD ON ENTRY                   00191502
                                      49 *        2. THE PSW MUST BE SUPERVISOR STATE,KEY 0                      00193502
                                      50 *                                                                       00197002
                                      51 *03*        CHARACTER-CODE-DEPENDENCIES = NONE                          00198502
                                      52 *                                                                       00208502
                                      53 *02*     RESTRICTIONS = THIS ROUTINE MAY BE INVOKED BY ONLY THE         00214802
                                      54 *        DISPATCHER. HE MUST OWN THE GLOBAL DISPATCHER LOCK ON ENTRY.   00216802
                                      55 *                                                                       00220702
                                      56 *02*     REGISTER-CONVENTIONS = SEE SEGMENT REGSTERS FOR DESCRIPTION OF 00221102

                                     255          TITLE 'SCHEDULING GLOBAL SRBS'                                 07110002
                                     256 *********************************************************************** 07120002
                                     257 *                                                                     * 07150002
                                     258 *   GLOBAL-                                                           * 07200002
                                     259 *        THIS ROUTINE IS THE MAINLINE CODE FOR SCHEDULING             * 07250002
                                     260 *        A GLOBAL PRIORITY SRB.                                         07300002
                                     261 *                                                                     * 07350002
                                     262 *        INPUT-                                                       * 07400002
                                     263 *          REGS-SEE PROLOGUE                                          * 07450002
                                     264 *                                                                     * 07500002
                                     265 *        FIELDS REF-                                                  * 07550002
                                     266 *              SRBFLNK-SRB FORWARD CHAIN FIELD                        * 07600002
                                     267 *              GLOBAL PRIORITY INDEX TABLE                            * 07650002
                                     268 *                                                                     * 07700002
                                     269 *        OUTPUT-                                                      * 07750002
                                     270 *          REGS-SEE PROLOGUE                                          * 07800002
                                     271 *                                                                     * 07850002
                                     272 *        FIELDS CHG-                                                  * 07900002
                                     273 *              NONE                                                   * 07950002
                                     274 *                                                                     * 08000002
                                     275 *        SUBROUTINES/LOCAL MACROS-                                    * 08050002
                                     276 *              QSRB- QUEUES AN SRB TO THE SPL                         * 08100002
                                     277 *              WAKEUP- SEARCHES FOR AND SIGNALS CPUS                  * 08150002
                                     278 *                   DISPATCHED TO THE WAIT ADDRESS SPACE              * 08200002
                                     279 *        SYSTEM INTERFACES-                                           * 08250002
                                     280 *              NONE                                                   * 08300002
                                     281 *                                                                     * 08350002
                                     282 *********************************************************************** 08360002
                                     283 */*SCH02: CHART GLOBAL SCHEDULE */                                      08361002
                                     284 */*IEAVESC1: E GLOBAL SCHEDULE */                                       08362002
                                     285 */*GLOOP1: P DEQUEUE THE TOP SRB */                                     08364002
                                     286 */* P JOURNAL REMAINDER OF THE QUEUE */                                 08365002
                                     287 */* P CALCULATE THE PRIORITY OF THE SRB */                              08366002
                                     288 */* S QSRB:QUEUE THE SRB TO THE SPL */                                  08367002
                                     289 */* D (YES,GLOOP1,NO,) ANY MORE SRB'S ON THE QUEUE? */                  08368002
                                     290 */* D (NO,NOWAIT,YES,) WAIT ASCB DISPATCHED? */                         08369002
                                     291 */* S WAKEUP:WAKE UP WAITING CPU'S */                                   08370002
                                     292 */*NOWAIT: R RETURN TO DISPATCHER */                                    08371002
                                     293 */*SCH02: END GLOBAL SCHEDULE */                                        08386002
                                     294          ENTRY IEAVESC1                                                 08392002
                                     295 IEAVESC1 DS    0H                                                       08400002
                                     296          USING *,R4                                             DSK1067 08450002
                                     297          LR    R2,R5      GET ADDR OF TOP SRB                   DSK1067 08500002
                                     298 GLOOP1   L     R5,SRBFLNK      DEQUEUE THE TOP SRB              DSK1067 08550002
                                     299          ST    R5,LCCASMQJ    JOURNAL QUEUE                     DSK1067 08560002
                                     300          SR    R15,R15    CLEAR REGISTER                        DSK1067 08600002
                                     301          ICM   R15,B4,SRBPRIOR GET THE PRIORITY VALUE           DSK1067 08650002
                                     302          L     R15,GPIT(R15)  GET THE ADDRESS OF THE            DSK1067 08700002
                                     303 *                                       PRIORITY LEVEL                  08750002
                                     304 *        QUEUE THE SRB TO THE SPL                                       08760002
                                     305 QGLOBL   QSRB  1                                                        08770002
                                     306          LTR   R2,R5       TEST FOR SRBS                        DSK1067 08850002
                                     307          BNZ   GLOOP1                IF MORE,GO GET THEM                08900002

0                                    320          MACRO                                                          09300002
                                     321          LOCAL                                                          09350002
                                     322 *********************************************************************** 09400002
                                     323 *   LOCAL-                                                              09450002
                                     324 *        THIS ROUTINE FINISHES THE SCHEDULING OF LOCAL                  09500002
                                     325 *        PRIORITY SRBS.   IT IS ENTERED FROM THE DISPATCHER AT ENTRY    09550002
                                     326 *        POINT IEAVESC2.  IT MOVES SRBS FROM THE SERVICE MANAGER QUEUE  09600002
                                     327 *        TO THE CORRECT LOCAL SPL.  IT WILL ALSO NOTIFY                 09610002
                                     328 *        THE SRM WHEN AN SRB IS BEING SCHEDULED TO  A                   09620002
                                     329 *        SWAPPED OUT ADDRESS SPACE.  IT WILL CALL MEMORY SWITCH ON      09630002
                                     330 *        A SCHEDULE TO A SWAPPED IN ADDRESS SPACE.                      09640002
                                     331 *        INPUT-                                                         09650002
                                     332 *          REGS-SEE PROLOG                                              09700002
                                     333 *                                                                       09750002
                                     334 *          FIELDS-                                                      09800002
                                     335 *              SRBFLNK  -  FIELD USED FOR QUEUEING.                     09850002
                                     336 *              SRBPRIOR  -  INDICATES PRIORITY LEVEL OF SRB.            09900002
                                     337 *              SRBASCB    -  INDICATES THE ADDRESS SPACE BEING          09950002
                                     338 *                          SCHEDULED TO                                 09970002
                                     339 *                                                                       10050002
                                     340 *              LPIT  -  LOCAL PRIORITY INDEX TABLE.  THIS IS AN         10100002
                                     341 *                    INTERNAL TABLE WHICH INDICATES                     10110002
                                     342 *                     THE OFFSET OF A SPECIFIC PRIORITY LEVEL           10120002
                                     343 *                    IN THE LOCAL SPL.                                  10130002
                                     344 *              ASCBNOQ  -  WHEN THIS BIT IS ON, THE ASCB IS   NOT ON    10150002
                                     345 *                          THE IN-CORE QUEUE.  MEMORY SWITCH SHOULD NOT 10160002
                                     346 *                          BE CALLED.                                   10170002
                                     347 *              ASCBSPL  - CONTAINS THE ADDRESS OF THE LOCAL SPL.        10200002
                                     348 *              ASCBOUT  - WHEN FLAG IS ON, A USERRDY SYSEVENT SHOULD BE 10250002
                                     349 *                         ISSUED.                                       10270002
                                     350 *        OUTPUT                                                         10300002
                                     351 *          REGS-SEE PROLGO                                              10350002
                                     352 *        SUBROUTINE/LOCAL MACROS-                                       10400002
                                     353 *              QSRB                                                     10450002
                                     354 *              OPTINT                                                   10500002
                                     355 *              MEMSW                                                    10550002
                                     356 *********************************************************************** 10600002
                                     357 */*SCH03: CHART LOCAL SCHEDULE */                                       10600702
                                     358 */*IEAVESC2: E LOCAL SCHEDULE */                                        10601402
                                     359 */*LOCAL1: P DEQUEUE THE TOP SRB */                                     10603502
                                     360 */* P JOURNAL REMAINDER OF THE QUEUE */                                 10604202
                                     361 */* P FIND THE CORRECT LSPL */                                          10604902
                                     362 */* P GET ADDRESS OF THE CORRECT PROIRITY LEVEL */                      10605602
                                     363 */* S QSRB:QUEUE SRB TO LSPL */                                         10606302

                                     737+*                                                                       00114002
                                     738+*01*  MODULE-NAME = IEAVESC0                                            00116002
                                     739+*                                                                       00118802
                                     740+*02*     CSECT-NAME = IEAVESC1                                          00119202
                                     741+*                                                                       00119702
                                     742+*01*  DESCRIPTIVE-NAME = SCHEDULE SERVICE ROUTINE                       00119802
                                     743+*                                                                       00129902
                                     744+*01*  COPYRIGHT = NONE                                                  00139902
                                     745+*01*  STATUS = VS2, RELEASE 2.0                                         00146402
                                     746+*                                                                       00146602
                                     747+*01*  FUNCTION = THE FUNCTION OF THIS MODULE IS TO COMPLETE THE         00159902
                                     748+*     SCHEDULING OPERATION INITIATED BY THE SCHEDULE MACRO. IT MOVES    00169902
                                     749+*     SRBS TO THE APPROPRIATE SERVICE PRIORITY LIST (SPL) AT THE        00170302
                                     750+*     SPECIFIED PRIORITY LEVEL. IT ALSO HANDLES OTHER FUNCTIONS         00170702
                                     751+*     INCLUDED IN INTRODUCING DISPATCHABLE WORK INTO THE SYSTEM,WHICH   00171102
                                     752+*     INCLUDES:                                                         00171502
                                     753+*        1. ALERTING A CPU DISPATCHED TO THE WAIT ADDRESS SPACE THAT    00171602
                                     754+*        THERE IS WORK TO BE DISPATCHED.                                00171702
                                     755+*        2. CALLING THE MEMORY SWITCH ROUTINE WHEN MAKING A SWAPPED-IN  00171802
                                     756+*        ADDRESS SPACE READY.                                           00173302
                                     757+*        3. CALLING THE SRM WHEN A SWAPPED-OUT ADDRESS SPACE IS         00175302
                                     758+*        READY.                                                         00177302
                                     759+*                                                                       00177502
                                     760+*02*     OPERATION =                                                    00179002
                                     761+*        GLOBAL SCHEDULE-                                               00181002
                                     762+*          1. INPUT SRBS ARE QUEUED TO THE GLOBAL SPL.                  00183002
                                     763+*          2. IF THE WAIT ADDRESS SPACE IS DISPATCHED,THEN EVERY CPU    00183402
                                     764+*          DISPATCHED TO THE WAIT ADDRESS SPACE WILL BE SIGNALLED VIA   00183802
                                     765+*          THE RPSGNL FUNCTION.                                         00184202
                                     766+*                                                                       00184602
                                     767+*        LOCAL SCHEDULE -                                               00184702
                                     768+*          1. INPUT SRBS WILL BE QUEUED TO THE CORRECT LOCAL SPL.       00184802
                                     769+*          2.  IF AN SRB IS BEING SCHEDLED TO AN ADDRESS SPACE THAT IS  00184902
                                     770+*          ON THE IN-CORE QUEUE, THEN MEMORY SWITCH WILL BE INVOKED.    00185302
                                     771+*          3. IF AN SRB IS BEING SCHEDULED TO A SWAPPED-OUT ADDRESS     00185702
                                     772+*          SPACE,  THEN A SYSEVENT WILL BE ISSUED TO NOTIFY THE         00185802
                                     773+*          SRM THAT THE ADDRESS SPACE CAN BE SWAPPED IN.                00185902
                                     774+*                                                                       00186102
                                     775+*                                                                       00186502
                                     776+*01*   NOTES =                                                          00186602
                                     777+*                                                                       00188002
                                     778+*02*     DEPENDENCIES =                                                 00189502
                                     779+*        1. THE DISPATCHER LOCK MUST BE HELD ON ENTRY                   00191502
                                     780+*        2. THE PSW MUST BE SUPERVISOR STATE,KEY 0                      00193502
                                     781+*                                                                       00197002
                                     782+*03*        CHARACTER-CODE-DEPENDENCIES = NONE                          00198502
                                     783+*                                                                       00208502
                                     784+*02*     RESTRICTIONS = THIS ROUTINE MAY BE INVOKED BY ONLY THE         00214802
                                     785+*        DISPATCHER. HE MUST OWN THE GLOBAL DISPATCHER LOCK ON ENTRY.   00216802
                                     786+*                                                                       00220702

                                     942+*                                                                       00300002
                                     943+*/* **************************************************************** */ 00350002
                                     944+*/*                                                                  */ 00400002
                                     945+*/*            WORK/SAVE AREA VECTOR TABLES (WSAVT)                  */ 00450002
                                     946+*/*                                                                  */ 00500002
                                     947+*/*  OS/VS2 SU64, 10/24/78, LEVEL=6                                  */ 00550064
                                     948+*/*                                                                  */ 00600002
                                     949+*/*  METHOD OF ACCESS                                                */ 00650002
                                     950+*/*      PL/S - INCLUDE IHAASXB TO DEFINE ASXBSPSA FOR LOCAL WSAVT.  */ 00700002
                                     951+*/*             INCLUDE CVT TO DEFINE CVTSPSA FOR GLOBAL WSAVT.      */ 00750002
                                     952+*/*             INCLUDE IHALCCA TO DEFINE LCCACPUS FOR CPU WSAVT.    */ 00800002
                                     953+*/*                                                                  */ 00850002
                                     954+*/*             ALL 3 WSAVT'S ARE MAPPED UNLESS MACRO VARIABLE(S)    */ 00900002
                                     955+*/*             ARE SET TO NO.  CODING %WSALOCAL='NO' BEFORE INCLUDE */ 00950002
                                     956+*/*             MEANS THAT A MAPPING OF LOCAL WSAVT WILL NOT BE      */ 01000002
                                     957+*/*             PRODUCED.  %WSAGLOBL='NO' SUPPRESSES GLOBAL WSAVT    */ 01050002
                                     958+*/*             MAPPING.  %WSACPU='NO' SUPPRESSES CPU WSAVT MAPPING. */ 01100002
                                     959+*/*                                                                  */ 01150002
                                     960+*/*      BAL -  THERE ARE 4 KEYWORD PARAMETERS AS FOLLOWS -          */ 01200002
                                     961+*/*                                                                  */ 01250002
                                     962+*/*             1. CLASS=LOCAL/GLOBAL/CPU WITH A DEFAULT OF LOCAL.   */ 01300002
                                     963+*/*                THIS PARAMETER DETERMINES WHICH WSAVT MAPPING IS  */ 01350002
                                     964+*/*                PRODUCED.                                         */ 01400002
                                     965+*/*                                                                  */ 01450002
                                     966+*/*             2. DSECT=YES/NO WITH A DEFAULT OF YES.  IF YES, A    */ 01500002
                                     967+*/*                DSECT IS PRODUCED FOR MAPPING REQUESTED BY CLASS  */ 01550002
                                     968+*/*                PARAMETER.  THE DSECT NAMES ARE -                 */ 01600002
                                     969+*/*                     WSAL - LOCAL WSAVT                           */ 01650002
                                     970+*/*                     WSAG - GLOBAL WSAVT                          */ 01700002
                                     971+*/*                     WSAC - CPU WSAVT                             */ 01750002
                                     972+*/*                                                                  */ 01800002
                                     973+*/*             3. FUNC=INIT.  IF THIS PARAMETER IS SPECIFIED, AN    */ 01850002
                                     974+*/*                INITIALIZED TABLE OF HALF-WORD LENGTH VALUES      */ 01900002
                                     975+*/*                DESCRIBING THE NUMBER OF ENTRIES AND LENGTH OF    */ 01950002
                                     976+*/*                EACH ENTRY IN WSAVT SPECIFIED BY CLASS PARAMETER  */ 02000002
                                     977+*/*                IS PRODUCED.                                      */ 02050002
                                     978+*/*                                                                  */ 02052002
                                     979+*/*             4. ACR=YES/NO WITH A DEFAULT OF YES.  IF CLASS=CPU   */ 02060002
                                     980+*/*                AND DSECT=NO, ACR=YES CREATES A WORK/SAVE AREA    */ 02070002
                                     981+*/*                FOR ACR, AND ACR=NO CREATES A RESERVED 8-BYTE     */ 02080002
                                     982+*/*                AREA BECAUSE ACR IS NOT IN THE SYSTEM.            */ 02090002
                                     983+*/*                                                                  */ 02100002
                                     984+*/*             DSECT=NO MUST BE SPECIFIED IF FUNC=INIT IS SPECIFIED.*/ 02150002
                                     985+*/*                                                                  */ 02200002
                                     986+*/*             IF DSECT=NO IS SPECIFIED AND FUNC=INIT IS NOT        */ 02250002
                                     987+*/*             SPECIFIED, THE MAPPING OF WSAVT WILL BE FOLLOWED BY  */ 02260002
                                     988+*/*             MAPPING OF ACTUAL WORK/SAVE AREAS.                   */ 02350002
                                     989+*/*                                                                  */ 02400002
                                     990+*/* **************************************************************** */ 02450002

                                    1032+*/*           WHERE N IS AN INTEGER BETWEEN 2 AND 3,INCLUSIVE.      */  00346002

                                    1037+*/*                                                                 */  00349602

                                    3787+*   LOCAL-                                                              09450002
                                    3788+*        THIS ROUTINE FINISHES THE SCHEDULING OF LOCAL                  09500002
                                    3789+*        PRIORITY SRBS.   IT IS ENTERED FROM THE DISPATCHER AT ENTRY    09550002
                                    3790+*        POINT IEAVESC2.  IT MOVES SRBS FROM THE SERVICE MANAGER QUEUE  09600002
                                    3791+*        TO THE CORRECT LOCAL SPL.  IT WILL ALSO NOTIFY                 09610002
                                    3792+*        THE SRM WHEN AN SRB IS BEING SCHEDULED TO  A                   09620002
                                    3793+*        SWAPPED OUT ADDRESS SPACE.  IT WILL CALL MEMORY SWITCH ON      09630002
                                    3794+*        A SCHEDULE TO A SWAPPED IN ADDRESS SPACE.                      09640002
                                    3795+*        INPUT-                                                         09650002
                                    3796+*          REGS-SEE PROLOG                                              09700002
                                    3797+*                                                                       09750002
                                    3798+*          FIELDS-                                                      09800002
                                    3799+*              SRBFLNK  -  FIELD USED FOR QUEUEING.                     09850002
                                    3800+*              SRBPRIOR  -  INDICATES PRIORITY LEVEL OF SRB.            09900002
                                    3801+*              SRBASCB    -  INDICATES THE ADDRESS SPACE BEING          09950002
                                    3802+*                          SCHEDULED TO                                 09970002
                                    3803+*                                                                       10050002
                                    3804+*              LPIT  -  LOCAL PRIORITY INDEX TABLE.  THIS IS AN         10100002
                                    3805+*                    INTERNAL TABLE WHICH INDICATES                     10110002
                                    3806+*                     THE OFFSET OF A SPECIFIC PRIORITY LEVEL           10120002
                                    3807+*                    IN THE LOCAL SPL.                                  10130002
                                    3808+*              ASCBNOQ  -  WHEN THIS BIT IS ON, THE ASCB IS   NOT ON    10150002
                                    3809+*                          THE IN-CORE QUEUE.  MEMORY SWITCH SHOULD NOT 10160002
                                    3810+*                          BE CALLED.                                   10170002
                                    3811+*              ASCBSPL  - CONTAINS THE ADDRESS OF THE LOCAL SPL.        10200002
                                    3812+*              ASCBOUT  - WHEN FLAG IS ON, A USERRDY SYSEVENT SHOULD BE 10250002
                                    3813+*                         ISSUED.                                       10270002
                                    3814+*        OUTPUT                                                         10300002
                                    3815+*          REGS-SEE PROLGO                                              10350002
